summary of the EPAR summary for public
Ifirmasta1 Irbesartanum
this document is a summary of the European Public Assessment Report (EPAR) for Imphiramast.
explains how the Committee on Medicines Medicinal Products for Human Use (CHMP) assessed this medicine to make its opinion on the granting of marketing authorisation and recommendations on the conditions of use of Ifirmasta.
, what's Ifirmasta?
Ifirsta is a medicine that contains the active substance irbesartan.
tablets are available in tablets (75, 150 and 300 mg).
Ifirsta is a ‘generic medicine’.
that Ifirmasta is similar to the ‘reference medicine’ already authorised in the European Union, and Aprovel.
more information on generic issues and answers will be found in the relevant document.
how is Ifiramasta used for?
Ifirmasta is used in adults with essential hypertension (in a shock blood pressure).
, “sternal” means that hypertension has no obvious cause.
Ifirsta is also used to treat kidney disease in adults with hypertension and diabetes 2.
